> 36: export function useAgentEconomics(daysBack = 7) {
> 37:   return useQuery({
> 38:     queryKey: ["agent_economics", daysBack],
> 39:     staleTime: 30_000,
> 40:     queryFn: async (): Promise<{
> 41:       executions: (AgentExecutionRow & { agent_name?: string; squad?: string | null })[];
> 42:       summary: AgentEconomicsSummary;
> 43:     }> => {
> 44:       const since = new Date();
> 45:       since.setDate(since.getDate() - daysBack);
> 46: 
> 47:       const { data: execs, error } = await supabase
> 48:         .from("agent_executions")
> 49:         .select("*, crew_agents(name,squad)")
> 50:         .gte("started_at", since.toISOString())
> 51:         .order("started_at", { ascending: false })
> 52:         .limit(500);
> 53: 
> 54:       if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
> 55:       const rows = (execs ?? []) as Array<
> 56:         AgentExecutionRow & { crew_agents?: { name: string; squad: string | null } | null }
> 57:       >;
> 58: 
> 59:       const flat = rows.map((r) => ({
> 60:         ...r,
> 61:         agent_name: r.crew_agents?.name,
> 62:         squad: r.crew_agents?.squad ?? null,
> 63:       }));
> 64: 
> 65:       const done = flat.filter((r) => r.status === "done");
> 66:       const summary: AgentEconomicsSummary = {
> 67:         total_executions: done.length,
> 68:         total_tokens_in: done.reduce((a, r) => a + (r.tokens_in || 0), 0),
> 69:         total_tokens_out: done.reduce((a, r) => a + (r.tokens_out || 0), 0),
> 70:         total_cost_usd_micro: done.reduce((a, r) => a + (r.cost_usd_micro || 0), 0),

> 5: RETURNS TRIGGER

> 8: SET search_path = public
> 9: AS $$
> 10: DECLARE
> 11:   v_key text;

> 19:   FROM vault.decrypted_secrets
> 20:   WHERE name = 'sb_secret_key'

> 22:

> 26:     RETURN NEW;
> 27:   END IF;
> 28:

> 30:     url     := 'http://kong:8000/functions/v1/aios-chat-gateway',

> 35:     ),
> 36:     body    := jsonb_build_object('record', row_to_json(NEW))
> 37:   );
> 38: 
> 39:   RETURN NEW;
> 40: EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
> 41:   RAISE WARNING '[aios-chat-gateway] trigger skipped for message %: %', NEW.id, SQLERRM;
> 42:   RETURN NEW;
> 43: END;
> 44: $$;
> 45: 
> 46: DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_aios_chat_gateway_on_insert ON public.aios_messages;
> 47: CREATE TRIGGER trg_aios_chat_gateway_on_insert
> 48:   AFTER INSERT ON public.aios_messages

  "CodeContent": "\"# Walkthrough \u2014 Pantheon Telemetry Integration\\n\\n## Goal\\n\\nIntegrate the 7 Pantheon agents (main, engineer, scientist, artisan, marketing-growth, guardian, claw-master) into the mcoCoin metering pipeline, providing cost attribution and a real-time visual HUD on the dashboard.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Changes Made\\n\\n### Database \u2014 Migration `20260606230000_pantheon_telemetry_setup.sql`\\n\\n[Migration file](file:///home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/supabase/migrations/20260606230000_pantheon_telemetry_setup.sql)\\n\\n- **7 rows** seeded into `agent_pricing` for `provider='openclaw'`, models `openclaw/main` through `openclaw/claw-master`\\n  - `price_in_cents_per_mtok = 59`, `price_out_cents_per_mtok = 79` (conservative, profit-capturing)\\n- **7 skeleton rows** seeded into `crew_agents` with `squad='pantheon'`, `agent_key` prefixed `pantheon-`\\n  - Uses `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` for idempotency (safe to re-run)\\n\\n---\\n\\n### Backend \u2014 `aios-chat-gateway` Edge Function\\n\\n[Gateway source](file:///home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/supabase/functions/aios-chat-gateway/index.ts)\\n\\nKey modifications:\\n\\n1. **Dynamic agent resolution** \u2014 resolves `agent_key` from `crew_agents` for the current `user_id` + `agent_key`; seeds on-demand if not found\\n2. **Execution lifecycle logging** \u2014 INSERTs into `agent_executions` with `status='running'` before the LLM call; UPDATEs to `completed`/`failed` after\\n3. **Cost computation** \u2014 calls `calc_agent_cost_mcoin` RPC with real `tokens_in`/`tokens_out` from the LLM response; stores `cost_usd_micro` and `cost_mcoin`\\n4. **mcoCoin deduction** \u2014 on success, calls `deduct_mco_coins` RPC to charge the user\\n\\n---\\n\\n### Frontend \u2014 Hook\\n\\n[usePantheonTelemetry.ts](file:///home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/src/hooks/usePantheonTelemetry.ts)\\n\\n- Queries `agent_executions` for the last 30 days where `provider='openclaw'`\\n- Aggregates per-agent: total executions, avg latency, total mcoCoin spent, success rate\\n- Computes fleet totals and trend data for the sparkli\n<truncated 1548 bytes>",

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

The user is reporting two critical issues:

1. **AIOS chat not working** - The frontend is making a direct call to `generativelanguage.googleapis.com` with an API key exposed in the URL, getting a 404 on the OPTIONS preflight (CORS issue). This means the chat is calling the Gemini API directly from the browser instead of going through the Edge Function `aios-chat-gateway`.

2. **API key leak** - The Gemini API key `AIzaSyDP3c9tuFRtc6ky5ME4j6P6WZ2hh1Vjw-o` is being exposed in the browser's network tab, which is a severe security vulnerability. GitGuardian also flagged another issue with a hardcoded password.


3. **GitGuardian alert** - There's a hardcoded password in `scripts/qa/test-whatsapp-webhook.ts` that was already fixed by switching to a random UUID.

Now I need to trace through the frontend code to find where the AIOS chat is making its direct API call instead of routing through the Edge Function, and locate where the API key is being exposed in the code.

</details>

- **Linha 131-143**: Fallback para OpenRouter, também direto do browser
- **Linha 144-146**: Só chama Edge Function (`vaultFallback`) se **nenhuma key** estiver presente

A fix é simples: **sempre usar a Edge Function** como canal primário. Vou verificar o `.env` e o `aios-chat-gateway`:

## 🤖 Gemini *(22:19:21)*

Criei o plano de remediação. Aqui vai o resumo dos dois problemas e a proposta:

## 🔴 Diagnóstico

1. **API Keys vazando no browser** — `.env` usa `VITE_GEMINI_API_KEY` e `VITE_OPENROUTER_API_KEY`. O Vite injeta TUDO que começa com `VITE_` no bundle JS do cliente. Qualquer pessoa com F12 vê as chaves.

2. **AIOS chamando Google diretamente** — `useAIOSChat.ts` lê essas keys e faz `fetch("https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/...")` direto do browser, passando a key na URL. O preflight CORS falha (404) porque Google não aceita `origin: https://login.mcorch.com`.

## 🛡️ Solução proposta

- **Remover `VITE_*` API keys** do `.env` (renomear para server-only sem prefixo)
- **Toda chamada LLM roteada via Edge Function** — o browser nunca toca nas API keys
- **Novo Edge Function `aios-chat-stream`** com SSE streaming para manter a UX do chat fluida
- **Live Mode (WebSocket)** desabilitado em produção (não há como proxiar WebSocket bidireccional de áudio)

O plano completo está no artefato. Preciso da sua aprovação antes de executar — especialmente sobre o **Live Mode** (desabilitar ou manter com fetch da key on-demand).

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

**Prioritizing Function Design**

I've decided to prioritize designing the `aios-chat-stream` Edge Function before building it. The function will need to accept POST requests, return CORS headers for OPTIONS requests, and verify the incoming data. This structured approach helps ensure a robust design before implementation.


**Refining SSE Stream Logic**

I'm now focusing on how to handle Gemini's SSE stream within the Edge Function. The initial plan involves fetching and forwarding chunks in real-time, but now, I am looking more closely at how the actual SSE stream works. I'm exploring the use of `TransformStream` to process each chunk or reading the stream using a reader. As I process each chunk, I want to decode the data, extract the delta text, accumulate the full response, and, once the stream ends, count tokens and perform logging.


**Clarifying Streaming Protocols**

Now, I'm defining a clear SSE protocol for the Edge Function. Each chunk can be `data: {"delta": "..."}\n\n` or `data: <delta_content>\n\n`, allowing a simpler format. This allows me to proxy the Gemini stream with minimal changes to the client-side parsing in `useAIOSChat.ts`. I also verified that the OpenAI compatible endpoint supports streaming, but the standard Gemini API streamGenerateContent is simpler to parse.

</details>

  "CodeContent": "\"import { createClient } from \\\"https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2\\\";\\n\\nconst corsHeaders = {\\n  \\\"Access-Control-Allow-Origin\\\": \\\"*\\\",\\n  \\\"Access-Control-Allow-Methods\\\": \\\"POST, OPTIONS\\\",\\n  \\\"Access-Control-Allow-Headers\\\":\\n    \\\"authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type\\\",\\n};\\n\\nconst SUPPORTED_GEMINI_MODELS = new Set([\\n  \\\"gemini-2.5-flash\\\",\\n  \\\"gemini-2.5-pro\\\",\\n  \\\"gemini-2.0-flash-001\\\",\\n  \\\"gemini-2.0-flash\\\",\\n  \\\"gemini-2.0-flash-exp\\\",\\n  \\\"gemini-2.5-pro-preview-03-25\\\",\\n  \\\"gemini-1.5-pro\\\",\\n  \\\"gemini-1.5-flash\\\",\\n]);\\n\\ninterface ChatMessage {\\n  role: \\\"user\\\" | \\\"assistant\\\" | \\\"system\\\";\\n  content: string;\\n}\\n\\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\\n  if (req.method === \\\"OPTIONS\\\") {\\n    return new Response(\\\"ok\\\", { headers: corsHeaders });\\n  }\\n\\n  const respond = (body: unknown, status = 200) =>\\n    new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {\\n      status,\\n      headers: { ...corsHeaders, \\\"Content-Type\\\": \\\"application/json\\\" },\\n    });\\n\\n  // Zero-Trust: require valid JWT\\n  const authHeader = req.headers.get(\\\"Authorization\\\");\\n  if (!authHeader?.startsWith(\\\"Bearer \\\")) {\\n    return respond({ error: \\\"Unauthorized\\\" }, 401);\\n  }\\n\\n  const supabase = createClient(\\n    Deno.env.get(\\\"SUPABASE_URL\\\")!,\\n    (Deno.env.get(\\\"SB_PUBLISHABLE_KEY\\\") ?? Deno.env.get(\\\"SUPABASE_ANON_KEY\\\"))!,\\n    { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } }\\n  );\\n\\n  const { data: { user }, error: userError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();\\n  if (userError || !user) {\\n    return respond({ error: \\\"Unauthorized\\\" }, 401);\\n  }\\n\\n  // Resolve user-level overrides: key and model preference\\n  const admin = createClient(\\n    Deno.env.get(\\\"SUPABASE_URL\\\")!,\\n    (Deno.env.get(\\\"SB_SECRET_KEY\\\") ?? Deno.env.get(\\\"SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\\\"))!,\\n  );\\n  const { data: userKeys } = await admin\\n    .from(\\\"decrypted_user_api_keys\\\")\\n    .select(\\\"google_api_key, pref_ai_model\\\")\\n    .eq(\\\"user_id\\\", user.id)\\n    .maybeSingle();\\n\\n  // User key takes precedence over vault\\n  c\n<truncated 7538 bytes>",
